### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de integrarla, con historia lineal y limpia, siempre que nadie más la use. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, porque reescribir commits que otros tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、不丢不重地到达；UDP 不做这些保证，只管尽快发出去，换来的是更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local work before you share it, to keep history linear and tidy. Merge once a branch is shared, because rebasing rewrites commits that others may already be building on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/user.test.ts
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，也可以写一段路径关键字，Bun 会匹配所有路径里含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test user
```

要只跑某个测试用例（不是整个文件），用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "登录失败"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change before it is applied to the actual data, so a database can replay or undo those records after a crash and recover to a consistent state. Because appending to a log is cheap and sequential, this gives durability without having to write every changed data page to disk on each commit.
```

  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京 → 上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="lowest">还没看</div>

  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="rows"><div class="k-empty">还没有班次，每天早上看完会更新在这里。</div></div>
  </div>

<script>
(function () {
  var rowsEl = document.getElementById('rows');
  function render(d) {
    if (!d || !d.trains) return;
    var under = d.trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    if (under.length) {
      var low = under.reduce(function (a, b) { return a.price <= b.price ? a : b; });
      document.getElementById('lowest').textContent = '最低 ' + low.no + ' ' + low.price + ' 元';
    }
    rowsEl.innerHTML = '';
    d.trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no + (t.price < 600 ? '' : '（超 600）');
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = t.seats || '';
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = t.price + ' 元';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      rowsEl.appendChild(row);
    });
    if (d.updated) document.getElementById('updated').textContent = '更新于 ' + d.updated;
  }
  if (window.ducter) {
    ducter.get('fare').then(render);
    ducter.onChange(function () { ducter.get('fare').then(render); });
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fare":"{\"updated\":\"第 3 天\",\"trains\":[{\"no\":\"G1\",\"price\":553,\"seats\":\"二等座余 12 张\"},{\"no\":\"G3\",\"price\":553,\"seats\":\"二等座余 4 张\"},{\"no\":\"G7\",\"price\":626,\"seats\":\"二等座\"}]}"}

```

(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done. The board is up, with Nights 2 and 3 filled in (182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, no stalls). Each night's result will add a row.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1709 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="sv">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="ss">writes per second, p99 latency</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n/1000) + 'k' : String(n); }
function render(){
  var runs = ducter.get('runs') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!runs.length) {
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = 'Each night’s result will list here.';
    list.appendChild(e);
    document.getElementById('sv').className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
    document.getElementById('sv').textContent = 'Not run yet';
    document.getElementById('ss').textContent = 'writes per second, p99 latency';
    return;
  }
  runs.forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + r.name + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(r.writes) + ' /s</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('sv').className = 'k-sum-value';
  document.getElementById('sv').textContent = fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('ss').textContent = last.name + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms';
  ducter.summary(last.name + ': ' + fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s, p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"name":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"name":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

  const what = $('#what').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat($('#amt').value);
  if (!what || !(amount >= 0)) return;
  if (editId) {
    items = items.map(i => i.id === editId ? {...i, day, what, amount} : i);
  } else {
    items.push({id: uid(), day, what, amount});
  }
  lastDay = day;
  ducter.set(KEY, items);
  resetForm();
  render();
}

$('#save').onclick = save;
$('#cancel').onclick = resetForm;
$('#del').onclick = () => {
  if (!editId) return;
  items = items.filter(i => i.id !== editId);
  ducter.set(KEY, items);
  resetForm();
  render();
};
$('#list').onclick = e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const it = items.find(i => i.id === row.dataset.id);
  if (!it) return;
  editId = it.id;
  $('#day').value = it.day === '未定日期' ? '' : it.day;
  $('#what').value = it.what;
  $('#amt').value = it.amount;
  $('#formTitle').textContent = '改这一笔';
  $('#cancel').style.display = '';
  $('#del').style.display = '';
  $('#addbox').open = true;
  $('#what').focus();
};

function load(v) {
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
ducter.onChange(v => load(v));
resetForm();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="what">花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="what" type="text"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="yuan">金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" type="text" placeholder="2026-11-23"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记上</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  let sum=0;
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    sum+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    row.innerHTML='<div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.what)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+'</div><div class="k-row-value">¥'+(Number(x.yuan)||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")+'</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  document.getElementById("total").textContent="¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length? xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const xs=(ducter.get("expenses")||[]).slice();
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

<main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value k-none" id="total">还没记</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记下第一笔就有合计</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="北京出差"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const money=n=>"¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("en-US",{maximumFractionDigits:2});
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.amt)||0),0);
  $("total").textContent= … ame="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销，点下面「记一笔」添第一笔";list.appendChild(e);return}
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.trip||"出差";
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";
    const c=document.createElement("span");c.textContent=x.cat||"";
    const d=document.createElement("span");d.textContent=x.day||"";
    s.append(c,d);
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=money(x.amt);
    r.append(m,s,v);list.appendChild(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total));
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(o=>o.setAttribute("aria-pressed",o===b?"true":"false"));
});
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"出差",day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";$("day").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

决定：在现有的 TTL 修复分支上继续做，用同一个假时钟。
完成标准：新增一条测试，把时钟推到恰好等于过期时间的那一刻，检查该值此时已经过期（或按现有 TTL 语义判定的边界行为）；跑整个测试文件，并把结果报告出来；连跑几次确认不抖。
不要做：不合并，不推送，不开 PR；不改别的测试。
地点：~/dev/foo，同一个仓库和分支。
